### [97] [Surface Response, Plasma Modes of coated Multi-Layered anisotropic Semi-Dirac Heterostructures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05097)
*Teresa Lee,Godfrey Gumbs,Thi Nga Do,Andrii Iurov,Danhong Huang*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该研究推导了异质结构表面响应函数的闭式解析表达式，研究了二维材料涂层的多层结构，分析了倾斜半狄拉克材料的等离子体特性，并探讨了其在防护涂层中的应用潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究旨在建立多层二维材料异质结构的表面响应函数理论框架，修正传统介质中二维层色散方程的描述，为理解此类结构的等离子体激发行为提供理论基础。

Method: 采用麦克斯韦方程组和线性响应理论推导表面响应函数的闭式解析表达式；对倾斜半狄拉克材料的等离子体特性进行解析和数值分析；通过密度图展示损失函数，观察不同动量方向的各向异性行为。

Result: 获得了单层和双层结构在长波长极限下的等离子体色散闭式解析表达式；观察到两层或三层结构存在两个等离子体分支（同相光学模式和反相声学模式），其中光学模式强度更高；数值结果显示损失函数在不同动量方向呈现各向异性行为。

Conclusion: 该研究建立了多层二维材料异质结构表面响应函数的完整理论框架，修正了传统色散方程，为倾斜半狄拉克材料的等离子体特性提供了深入理解，在紫外防护、化学保护等耐久防护涂层领域具有应用潜力。

Abstract: We derived closed-form analytical expressions for the surface response functions (SRFs) for heterostructure. We investigate structures consisting of up to three layered, coated heterostructure of two-dimensional (2D) materials with a dielectric medium or vacuum interface. The dielectric media serves to inhibit charge transfer between layers for the case when a pair of 2D layers serve as coatings for a dielectric film. Our results revise the established picture for the dispersion equation for two layers of reduced dimensionality surrounded by dielectric media. An impinging electromagnetic field incident on the surface leads to Coulomb coupled plasma excitations in the structure which are yielded by the SRF. This is achieved by employing Maxwell's equations and linear response theory. We use these results to investigate the plasmonic properties of tilted semi-Dirac materials both analytically and numerically. Closed-form analytical expressions are derived for the plasmon dispersions in the long wavelength limit for single and double layers. We numerically obtain density plots of the loss functions and observe anisotropic behavior in different momentum directions. For the cases when there are two or three layers, we observe two plasmon branches corresponding to in-phase and out-of-phase charge density oscillations, where the in-phase optical modes have higher intensity than the out-of-phase acoustic modes. We calculated the optical absorption spectra for plasma modes in layered semi-Dirac materials produced by an external electromagnetic field carrying an electric polarization and frequency. Possible applications include durable protection coatings providing UV resistance, chemical protection and improving upon traditional ceramic coatings.

</details>


### [98] [Valley polarization of chiral excitonic bound states induced by band geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05222)
*Archisman Panigrahi,Daniel Kaplan*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 研究范德华材料中贝里相位如何重塑激子配对，发现贝里通量可导致有限角动量激子态（包括手性态）成为基态，并在多层菱面体石墨烯模型中观察到角动量混合现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索范德华材料中相互作用、拓扑和配对电子相之间的关联，特别是贝里相位如何影响激子配对行为，为理解这些系统中的集体量子现象提供新视角。

Method: 1. 研究双阱色散系统中的激子配对，计算温度-贝里通量相图；2. 分析多层菱面体石墨烯模型，考虑三角扭曲效应；3. 比较贝里通量调控下的角动量态演化与氢原子在均匀磁场中的情况。

Result: 1. 发现参数范围内有限角动量激子（包括手性态）成为基态；2. 凝聚角动量通道随贝里通量演化，展现出与氢原子磁场中角动量态不同的交叉行为；3. 连续旋转对称性破缺后，激子混合多个角动量（s、p、f、g），基态呈现多种角动量线性组合。

Conclusion: 贝里相位显著重塑范德华材料中的激子配对，可能导致手性激子凝聚体和通过多体凝聚实现的自发对称性破缺，为探索新型量子相提供了可能性。

Abstract: Van der Waals (vdW) materials provide a rich platform for exploring the interplay of interactions, topology, and paired-electron phases. We study how the Berry phase reshapes excitonic pairing in a double-well dispersion representative of layered vdW systems. By computing the temperature-versus-Berry flux phase diagram of the system, we find parameter ranges where finite angular momentum excitons are favored, including chiral states. Strikingly, the condensed angular momentum channel evolves with Berry flux, revealing a pairing problem with no analogue in a hydrogen atom in a uniform magnetic field, where angular momentum states never cross. We then turn to a model of multilayer rhombohedral graphene and examine the effects of trigonal warping. Once continuous rotational symmetry is broken, excitons mix multiple angular momenta, and for a range of parameters we find a variety of linear combination of angular momenta ($s, p, f$ and $g$) in the ground state. Our results point to the possibility of chiral excitonic condensates, and spontaneous symmetry breaking through many-body condensation.

</details>


### [99] [Edge universality in Floquet sideband spectra](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05244)
*Miguel Tierz*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该论文研究了非相互作用费米子在单色相位驱动（Tien-Gordon机制）下，尖锐费米边沿处的出射边带占据由离散贝塞尔核支配，并在大振幅极限下收敛于随机矩阵理论的Airy核，具有直接输运后果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究Floquet系统中非平衡输运的普适性行为，特别是相位驱动下费米边沿的量子统计特性，探索从离散贝塞尔核到Airy核的标度极限及其在输运测量中的表现。

Method: 采用Tien-Gordon机制分析非相互作用费米子的单色相位驱动，通过线性探测链与Floquet散射矩阵的桥梁，利用对易门控分离单相干阶块的一体关联函数，推导出离散贝塞尔核的精确解。

Result: 证明出射边带占据由离散贝塞尔核精确描述，大振幅极限下在A^{1/3}标度上收敛于Airy核；光辅助散粒噪声斜率偏离高偏压平台的行为坍缩到Airy核对角线；确定了Airy标度保持尖锐的交叉温度。

Conclusion: 在Floquet系统中发现了从离散贝塞尔核到随机矩阵理论Airy核的普适标度极限，这一普适性直接体现在输运测量中，且多音驱动可访问Floquet-Sambe空间中的Pearcey核尖点。

Abstract: We show that, for non-interacting fermions under a monochromatic phase drive (Tien--Gordon regime), the outgoing sideband occupations at a sharp Fermi edge are governed by the discrete Bessel kernel -- an exact result at any drive amplitude~$A$. In the large-amplitude regime the edge of this kernel converges, on the $A^{1/3}$ scale, to the Airy kernel of random matrix theory. This universality has a direct transport consequence: the deficit of the photo-assisted shot-noise slope from its high-bias plateau collapses onto the Airy-kernel diagonal. The derivation rests on a bridge between the linear detection chain and the Floquet scattering matrix: commensurate gating isolates a single coherence-order block of the one-body correlator. We identify the crossover temperature below which the Airy scaling is sharp, extend the analysis to biased two-terminal occupations, and argue that multi-tone drives make Pearcey-kernel cusps accessible in Floquet--Sambe space.

</details>


### [100] [Spin-biased quantum spin Hall effect in altermagnetic Lieb lattice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05311)
*Qianjun Wang,Ruqian Wu,Jun Hu*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 在Lieb晶格中，中等电子关联即可产生反铁磁序，自旋轨道耦合驱动系统进入拓扑相，展现具有自旋偏置边缘态的新型量子自旋霍尔效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反铁磁序作为新发现的磁态，在自旋电子学和量子技术中具有应用潜力。研究旨在探索Lieb晶格中反铁磁序的拓扑特性及其潜在应用价值。

Method: 使用Hubbard模型理论研究了二维Lieb晶格中的反铁磁态，分析了电子关联和自旋轨道耦合对系统拓扑性质的影响。

Result: 中等电子关联即可产生反铁磁序；自旋轨道耦合驱动系统进入拓扑相，在一维纳米带中产生具有自旋偏置的新型量子自旋霍尔效应边缘态，这些边缘态具有不同的局域化和速度。

Conclusion: 反铁磁Lieb晶格中的新型自旋偏置量子自旋霍尔效应为基础研究和创新器件概念开辟了令人兴奋的机会，值得立即进行实验探索。

Abstract: Altermagnetic (AM) order, a recently discovered magnetic state, has attracted intense research interest for its potential applications in spintronic and quantum technologies. Here, we theoretically investigate the AM state in the Lieb lattice, a prototypical two-dimensional lattice, using the Hubbard model. We show that AM order emerges with only moderate electronic correlations. Strikingly, spin-orbit coupling drives the system into a topological phase exhibiting a new quantum spin Hall effect (QSHE) with spin-biased topological edge states in one-dimensional nanoribbons. These edge states possess different localizations and velocities, and hence may produce spin and charge currents, fundamentally distinct from that in conventional topological insulators with spin degeneracy. This novel spin-biased QSHE in the AM Lieb lattice unveils exciting opportunities for both fundamental studies and innovative device concepts, motivating immediate experimental exploration.

</details>


### [101] [Orbital-driven emergent transport in altermagnets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05322)
*Junyeong Choi,Kyoung-Whan Kim*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 将反铁磁哈密顿量扩展至包含轨道自由度作为动力学变量，推导出相应的涌现电磁场，展示了可通过晶格各向异性控制的涌现电场及其导致的轨道和磁多极电流。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反铁磁材料因其独特的磁对称性成为自旋电子学的重要平台，但现有研究主要关注自旋自由度，轨道自由度的动力学作用尚未得到充分探索。

Method: 将反铁磁哈密顿量扩展至包含轨道自由度作为动力学变量，推导相应的涌现电磁场理论框架，分析晶格各向异性、动态晶格畸变等因素对涌现电场的影响。

Result: 1) 证明了可通过晶格各向异性控制的涌现电场及其导致的轨道和磁多极电流；2) 发现在简化的自旋和轨道纹理中，即使存在动态晶格畸变也能产生非零的涌现电场；3) 该理论框架可推广至高阶反铁磁系统，超越d波体系。

Conclusion: 通过将轨道自由度纳入反铁磁哈密顿量，建立了包含轨道动力学的涌现电磁场理论，为反铁磁材料中的轨道调控和新型自旋电子学器件设计提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Altermagnets have recently emerged as a promising platform for spintronics due to their unique magnetic symmetry. However, most studies have focused on spin degrees of freedom, leaving the dynamic role of orbital degrees of freedom largely unexplored. In this work, we extend the altermagnet Hamiltonian to include the orbital degree of freedom as a dynamical variable and derive the resulting emergent electromagnetic fields (EEMFs). This approach allows us to demonstrate emergent electric fields controllable via lattice anisotropy and the resulting orbital and magnetic multipole currents. Furthermore, we show that non-vanishing emergent electric fields can arise even in simplified spin and orbital textures, particularly in the presence of dynamic lattice distortion. This formalism is generalizable to high-order altermagnets beyond d-wave systems.

</details>


### [102] [Nonlinear thermal gradient induced magnetization in $d^{\prime }$, $g^{\prime }$ and $i^{\prime }$ altermagnets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05454)
*Motohiko Ezawa*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该研究证明在不存在线性温度梯度的情况下，通过非线性温度梯度可以在特定类型的altermagnet中诱导出磁化，其中d'波、g'波、i'波altermagnet会产生响应，而d波、g波、i波altermagnet则不会。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索在非线性温度梯度作用下，是否能在没有线性温度梯度分量的情况下诱导出磁化，这一现象在理论上具有高度非平凡性，对于理解altermagnet的热电响应特性具有重要意义。

Method: 通过分析不同波对称性的altermagnet的能带结构特征，其中d波、g波、i波altermagnet的特征为k^N_X sin N_Xφ，而d'波、g'波、i'波altermagnet的特征为k^N_X cos N_Xφ。研究这些系统在二阶非线性温度梯度下的响应，并推导高温区域下的解析表达式。

Result: 在d'波、g'波、i'波altermagnet中，二阶非线性温度梯度可以诱导出有限磁化，而在d波、g波、i波altermagnet中则不会产生这种响应。这是主导阶贡献，因为线性响应被反演对称性禁止。同时发现p波、f波、p'波和f'波奇宇称磁体不会出现类似的非线性热响应。

Conclusion: 非线性温度梯度可以在特定对称性的altermagnet中诱导磁化，这一发现揭示了altermagnet中热电响应的新机制，为调控磁化提供了新的物理途径，并区分了不同对称性altermagnet的热响应特性。

Abstract: It is a highly nontrivial question whether a magnetization can be induced by applying a nonlinear temperature gradient in the absence of any linear component. In this work, we address this issue and provide explicit examples demonstrating that such a response can indeed arise. The spin-split band structures of $d$-wave, $g$-wave, $i$-wave altermagnets are characterized by $k^{N_{X}}\sin N_{X}φ$, where $N_{X}=2,4$ and $6$, respectively. In contrast, the corresponding $d^{\prime }$-wave, $g^{\prime } $-wave, $i^{\prime }$-wave altermagnets are described by $k^{N_{X}}\cos N_{X}φ$. We show that a finite magnetization is induced in the $d^{\prime }$-wave, $g^{\prime }$-wave, $i^{\prime }$-wave altermagnets under a second-order nonlinear temperature gradient, whereas no such response occurs in the $d$-wave, $g$-wave, $i$-wave altermagnets. This constitutes the leading-order contribution because the linear response is forbidden by inversion symmetry. Furthermore, we derive analytic expressions for the induced magnetization in the high-temperature regime. We also demonstrate that no analogous nonlinear thermal response appears in $p$-wave, $f$-wave, $p^{\prime }$-wave and $f^{\prime }$-wave odd-parity magnets.

</details>


### [103] [Room Temperature Anisotropic Photoresponse in Low-Symmetry van der Waals Semiconductor CrPS$_4$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05572)
*Cédric A. Cordero-Silis,Daniel Vaquero,Teresa López-Carrasco,Harshan Madeshwaran,Marcos H. D. Guimarães*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该研究揭示了二维材料CrPS₄在1.37-2.48 eV范围内的强光学和光电各向异性，其线性二向色性在反射中达~50，在光电流中达~60，且光电流沿b轴比a轴增强3倍，表明其是室温下具有强偏振敏感响应的各向异性半导体平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索低对称性二维材料的光学各向异性，利用其晶体和光学各向异性实现强二向色响应，为光子学和光电子器件提供增强的偏振对比度。

Method: 在1.37-2.48 eV范围内进行线性偏振反射率和扫描光电流测量，分析线性二向色性(RLD)和光电流线性二向色性(PCLD)，通过空间分辨光电流映射研究光电流对晶轴的依赖性，并基于Cr³⁺ d轨道T₁和T₂跃迁解释各向异性响应机制。

Result: 反射线性二向色性RLD达~50，光电流线性二向色性PCLD达~60，在1.6-1.8 eV间RLD出现符号反转；光电流沿b轴比a轴增强3倍，不同接触取向下光响应呈现清晰的180°调制，表明CrPS₄具有强室温偏振敏感响应。

Conclusion: CrPS₄是具有强线性二向色性和室温偏振敏感光电响应的各向异性二维半导体，为窄带偏振光电探测器、各向异性光传输以及未来二维自旋电子学和磁光器件提供了有前景的平台。

Abstract: The crystalline and optical anisotropy of low-symmetry two-dimensional (2D) materials can enable strong dichroic responses, enhancing polarization contrast for photonic and optoelectronic devices. Here, we unveil pronounced optical and optoelectronic anisotropy in chromium thiophosphate CrPS$_4$ arising from the strong coupling between light polarization and its intrinsic crystal symmetry. Linearly polarized reflectivity and scanning photocurrent measurements in the 1.37-2.48 eV range reveal a robust dichroic response. The linear dichroism in reflection RLD reaches ~50, while in photocurrent PCLD it increases to ~60, with a sign reversal of the RLD between 1.6-1.8 eV, enabling strong narrow-band polarization contrast at room temperature. We attribute these anisotropic responses to the interaction between polarized light and Cr$^{3+}$ d-orbital T$_1$ and T$_2$ transitions. Spatially resolved photocurrent mapping further shows that the photocurrent is strongly dependent on the crystallographic axis: a 3-fold enhancement is obtained along the b-axis compared to the a-axis, yielding a clear 180° modulation of photoresponse across different contact orientations. Together, our findings establish CrPS$_4$ as a highly anisotropic 2D semiconductor with strong linear dichroism and polarization-sensitive photoresponse at room temperature. These characteristics highlight CrPS4 as a promising platform for narrow-band polarized photodetectors, anisotropic photo-transport, and future 2D spintronic and magneto-optical devices.

</details>


### [104] [Robust quantized thermal conductance of Majorana floating edge bands in d-wave superconductors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05588)
*Yanmiao Han,Yu-Hao Wan,Zhaoqin Cao,Rundong Zhao,Qing-Feng Sun*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该论文提出并表征了一种新型马约拉纳边界态——浮动马约拉纳边缘带（FMEBs），这种态出现在破坏时间反演对称性的二维超导体中，但表现出类螺旋输运特性。与传统的螺旋或手性边缘模式不同，FMEBs形成孤立的、动量分离的反向传播马约拉纳模式，与体连续谱分离。论文通过非平衡格林函数模拟揭示了其独特的输运指纹：双端器件中的量子化总热导和四端几何中的鲁棒半量子化平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统拓扑超导体中的马约拉纳边缘态通常需要时间反演对称性（如拓扑绝缘体/超导体异质结）或表现为手性模式（如手性p波超导体）。然而，在破坏时间反演对称性的系统中实现类螺旋的马约拉纳输运仍然是一个挑战。本研究旨在发现并表征一种新型的马约拉纳边界态，它能在没有时间反演对称性的系统中实现螺旋状输运特性，为拓扑量子计算提供新的实现途径。

Method: 1. 理论提出：通过两带Bogoliubov-de Gennes模型中的各向异性Wilson质量机制，提出浮动马约拉纳边缘带（FMEBs）的概念
2. 微观实现：在d波超导体近邻耦合的量子反常霍尔绝缘体中实现FMEBs
3. 输运表征：使用非平衡格林函数（NEGF）模拟研究FMEBs的输运特性
4. 鲁棒性分析：考察有限温度、中等长程无序和有限化学势条件下的稳定性

Result: 1. 识别出FMEBs的清晰输运指纹：双端器件中的量子化总热导（κ = κ₀，其中κ₀是量子化热导单位）
2. 四端几何中观察到鲁棒的半量子化平台（κ = κ₀/2），这能明确区分FMEBs与手性N=±2量子反常霍尔相
3. FMEBs在有限温度、中等长程无序和有限化学势条件下表现出显著的稳定性
4. 建立了FMEBs作为在没有时间反演对称性系统中实现类螺旋马约拉纳输运的实验可行途径

Conclusion: 该研究提出并表征了一种新型的马约拉纳边界态——浮动马约拉纳边缘带（FMEBs），它在破坏时间反演对称性的二维超导体中实现类螺旋输运。FMEBs形成孤立的、动量分离的反向传播马约拉纳模式，具有独特的量子化输运特征。这种态在实验相关条件下保持稳定，为在没有时间反演对称性的系统中实现马约拉纳物理和拓扑量子计算提供了新的平台。四端几何中的半量子化热导平台可作为实验上明确识别FMEBs的清晰指纹。

Abstract: We propose and characterize a new class of Majorana boundary states, i.e., floating Majorana edge bands (FMEBs), which emerge in two-dimensional (2D) superconductors that break time-reversal symmetry yet host helical-like transport. In contrast to conventional chiral or helical edge modes, FMEBs form isolated, momentum-separated counterpropagating Majorana modes detached from the bulk continuum. We identify a minimal mechanism for their emergence via anisotropic Wilson masses in a two-band Bogoliubov-de Gennes (BdG) model, and demonstrate their microscopic realization in a quantum anomalous Hall (QAH) insulator proximitized by a $d$-wave superconductor. Using nonequilibrium Green's function (NEGF) simulations, we uncover clear transport fingerprints: a quantized total thermal conductance in two-terminal devices, and a robust half-quantized plateau in four-terminal geometries that cleanly distinguishes FMEBs from chiral $\mathcal{N}= \pm 2$ QAH phases. This thermal response remains remarkably stable under finite temperature, moderate long-range disorder, and finite chemical potential. Our findings establish FMEBs as an experimentally accessible route toward helical-like Majorana transport in systems without time-reversal symmetry, with direct implications for topological quantum computation.

</details>


### [105] [Indication of Stochastic Photothermal Dynamics around a Topological Defect in a Chiral Magnet](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05608)
*Dongxue Han,Asuka Nakamura,Takahiro Shimojima,Kosuke Karube,Yasujiro Taguchi,Yoshinori Tokura,Kyoko Ishizaka*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 利用泵浦-探测洛伦兹透射电子显微镜研究Co9Zn9Mn2中光热诱导的螺旋-顺磁相变，发现磁性边缘位错周围恢复动力学存在随机性增强


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手性磁体中拓扑保护的旋涡结构在非平衡动力学中起关键作用，但超快缺陷介导过程仍不清楚，需要研究拓扑缺陷对磁性相变恢复动力学的影响

Method: 使用泵浦-探测洛伦兹透射电子显微镜（LTEM）研究Co9Zn9Mn2中飞秒脉冲激光诱导的光热螺旋-顺磁相变，观察磁性条纹对比度抑制后的恢复过程

Result: 观察到由各向异性热扩散驱动的磁性有序定向恢复过程，磁性边缘位错周围恢复延迟且LTEM对比度出现瞬态模糊，表明恢复动力学通过随机选择的多重弛豫路径进行

Conclusion: 磁性边缘位错周围的恢复动力学表现出随机性增强，表明拓扑缺陷在磁性相变恢复过程中可能增强随机性选择机制

Abstract: Chiral magnets host topologically protected spin textures whose nonequilibrium dynamics are crucial in phase transitions and domain evolution, yet ultrafast defect-mediated processes remain poorly understood. Here, we investigate photothermally induced helical-to-paramagnetic phase transition in Co$_9$Zn$_9$Mn$_2$ using pump-probe Lorentz transmission electron microscopy (LTEM). Following the suppression of the magnetic stripe contrast induced by femtosecond pulsed laser, we observe a directional recovery process of magnetic order driven by the anisotropic thermal diffusion, toward the thick region that effectively acts as a heat sink. Remarkably, around a magnetic edge dislocation, the magnetic contrast recovery exhibits a pronounced delay accompanied by a transient blurring of LTEM contrast. These findings suggest that the recovery dynamics around the magnetic edge dislocation proceed through multiple relaxation paths that are selected stochastically. Our results indicate a possible enhancement of stochasticity around topological defects during the recovery dynamics of magnetic phase transitions.

</details>


### [106] [Two-Dimensional Space-Time Groups: Classification and Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05619)
*Chenhang Ke,Congjun Wu*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该论文提出了2+1维时空晶体的完整分类理论，基于群上同调方法识别了275种时空晶体（其中203种非简单），揭示了时空对称性带来的独特物理现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统空间群理论已不足以描述激光驱动晶格、动态光子/声子晶体等具有时空控制的新型材料体系，需要建立超越Floquet定理的时空群理论框架。

Method: 采用群上同调方法对2+1维时空群进行完整分类，识别所有可能的时空晶体对称性结构。

Result: 完整分类出275种时空晶体，其中203种具有非简单对称性；发现了手性选择响应规则，并预测了非简单时空对称性导致的"水平锥"结构。

Conclusion: 该工作为预测和设计凝聚态物理和超材料中的新型时空现象提供了理论基础，时空群框架将推动对动态材料体系的理解和应用。

Abstract: The concept of space group has long served as the fundamental framework to describe the physical properties of crystalline materials, from electronic bands to photonic dispersions. The recent progress of spatiotemporal control, such as laser-driven lattices, dynamic photonic and phononic crystals, and dynamic optical lattices, necessitates the study of a new framework, space-time group, beyond that based on the Floquet theorem. Space-time group includes novel intertwined non-symmorphic spatial-temporal symmetries such as time-glide reflection and time-screw rotation. Here, we perform a complete classification of the 2+1D space-time groups based on the method of group cohomology, leading to the identification of all 275 space-time crystals, including 203 non-symmorphic ones. Under this formalism, unique physical phenomena are uncovered: A chirality-selective response rule with specific space-time symmetry is fully investigated and a novel ``horizontal cone" structure is predicted in space-time metamaterials as a direct consequence of non-symmorphic space-time symmetry. This work serves as a starting point for predicting and engineering a wide range of novel spatiotemporal phenomena across condensed matter and metamaterials.

</details>


### [107] [Bias controlled Interlayer Exchange Coupling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05705)
*Nathan A. Walker,Alex D. Durie,Andrey Umerski*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 论文通过计算机模拟和非平衡格林函数方法证明，外加电偏压可以改变非平衡层间交换耦合的符号，实现铁磁三层结构中平行和反平行磁构型之间的电控切换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究电偏压对非平衡层间交换耦合的影响，探索通过电控方式切换铁磁多层结构磁构型的可能性，为低功耗磁存储和自旋电子器件提供理论基础。

Method: 采用计算机模拟和非平衡格林函数方法，研究由绝缘区连接的铁磁三层结构夹在半无限引线之间的系统。考虑三种绝缘区：单隧穿势垒、共振隧穿势垒和非晶绝缘势垒，分析系统电导对非平衡层间交换耦合偏压依赖性的影响。

Result: 当铁磁三层中包含量子阱态时，相对较小的外加电偏压可使三层的最低能态在平行和反平行构型之间切换。非平衡层间交换耦合的偏压依赖性强烈依赖于系统电导，强局域量子阱态可实现最低的切换电流密度。

Conclusion: 电偏压可有效调控非平衡层间交换耦合的符号，实现铁磁多层结构磁构型的电控切换。强局域量子阱态是实现低电流密度切换的关键，为设计低功耗磁存储器件提供了重要指导。

Abstract: We demonstrate, using computer simulations and a non-equilibrium Greens function approach, that the sign of the out-of-equilibrium interlayer exchange coupling (ooeIEC) can change in the presence of an externally applied electrical bias. Our system consists of an insulating section connected to an exchange coupled ferromagnetic (FM) tri-layer, sandwiched between semi-infinite leads. When the exchange coupled trilayer contains a quantum-well state confined in the hybridisation gap (HG) of the FM, we find that a relatively small applied electrical bias can switch the lowest energy state of the tri-layer between parallel (P) and anti-parallel (AP) configurations. We consider three cases for the insulating section; a single tunnelling barrier, a resonant tunnelling barrier and an amorphous insulating barrier and, in each case, show that the bias dependence of the ooeIEC is strongly dependent on the system conductance. We find that the lowest switching current densities are achieved with strongly confined quantum well states.

</details>


### [108] [Predicted DC current induced by propagating wave in gapless Dirac materials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05708)
*Keisuke Kitayama,Masao Ogata*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 传播波可在空间反演对称系统中诱导直流电流，通过微扰理论和Floquet理论推导方程，应用于石墨烯并预测非零直流电流


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究传播波在具有空间反演对称性的系统中诱导直流电流的可能性，探索传统对称性限制之外的电流产生机制

Method: 采用两种理论方法：微扰理论和Floquet理论推导传播波诱导直流电流的方程，并将理论应用于具有次近邻跃迁项的石墨烯系统

Result: 理论推导结果一致，预测在具有次近邻跃迁项的石墨烯中可产生非零直流电流，并讨论了强波幅下的非微扰效应

Conclusion: 传播波可在空间反演对称系统中诱导直流电流，这一发现扩展了对光电流产生机制的理解，为相关应用提供了理论基础

Abstract: In this paper, we show that the application of propagating waves can induce a DC current even in systems with spatial inversion symmetry. We derive the equation for the DC current induced by propagating waves using two methods: perturbation theory and Floquet theory. These two approaches yield consistent results. We then apply the equation to gapless graphene subjected to propagating waves. A nonzero DC current is predicted in graphene with next nearest neighbor hopping terms. Nonperturbative effects arising from a strong wave amplitude are also discussed within the framework of Floquet theory.

</details>


### [109] [Controlled dewetting and phase transition hysteresis of VO2 nanostructures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05796)
*Peter Kepič,Petra Kalousková,Tomáš Šikola,Filip Ligmajer*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该论文展示了通过光刻图案化、受控结晶和去湿技术控制VO₂纳米柱的相变，为可扩展、高能效的光子存储和神经形态器件提供了关键进展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着人工智能发展，需要更高效的数据处理方式。除了从电子电路转向光子电路外，集成相变材料是很有前景的途径。VO₂具有超快、近室温相变特性，表现出滞后效应和大光学调制，适合短期存储和神经形态计算。然而，VO₂的滞后行为主要在薄膜和纳米结构中研究，实际控制和器件集成仅限于薄膜，纳米结构的可控制造是瓶颈。

Method: 采用光刻图案化、受控结晶和受控去湿技术来制造VO₂纳米柱。通过这些方法实现对纳米柱几何形状和滞后特性的精确控制，使其能够直接集成到器件平台上。

Result: 成功实现了对VO₂纳米柱相变的控制，能够制造具有定制几何形状和滞后特性的纳米结构。纳米结构比薄膜更容易寻址且功耗更低，这种制造能力为可扩展的光子器件奠定了基础。

Conclusion: 通过光刻图案化、受控结晶和去湿技术控制VO₂纳米柱相变的能力，是实现可扩展、高能效存储和神经形态光子器件的关键步骤。这项工作解决了纳米结构制造和集成的挑战，为脑启发计算系统提供了重要进展。

Abstract: As artificial intelligence continues to grow, so does the need for more efficient ways to process data. Besides moving from electronic to photonic circuits, a promising approach is to integrate phase-change materials. Vanadium dioxide (VO$_2$) exhibits an ultrafast, near-room-temperature phase transition, characterized by hysteresis and large optical modulation -- making it a promising candidate for short-term memories and for mimicking neural behavior in brain-like computing systems. While the hysteresis behavior of VO$_2$ has been well studied in thin films and nanostructures, practical control and device integration have been limited only to thin films. Here, we demonstrate control over the phase transitions of VO$_2$ nanocylinders via lithographic patterning, controlled crystallization, and controlled dewetting. Because nanostructures are easier to address and consume less power than films, the ability to fabricate them with tailored geometry and hysteresis properties directly on integrated platforms is a key step toward scalable, energy-efficient memory and neuromorphic photonic devices.

</details>


### [110] [Interband optical conductivities in two-dimensional tilted Dirac bands revisited within the tight-binding model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05803)
*Chao-Yang Tan,Jian-Tong Hou,Xin Chen,Ling-Zhi Bai,Jie Lu,Yong-Hong Zhao,Chang-Xu Yan,Hao-Ran Chang,Hong Guo*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该理论研究二维倾斜狄拉克带中的带间纵向光学电导率，发现紧束缚模型存在三种特征临界频率，这些频率在线性化k·p模型中缺失，其中两种频率对带倾斜和狄拉克点移动具有鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究二维倾斜狄拉克系统中带倾斜和狄拉克点移动对光学性质的影响，特别是探索紧束缚模型与线性化k·p模型在描述光学响应时的差异，为实验研究提供理论指导。

Method: 在线性响应理论框架下，采用紧束缚模型研究二维倾斜狄拉克带的带间纵向光学电导率，结合带倾斜和狄拉克点移动效应，并使用拉格朗日乘子法推导解析表达式。

Result: 在紧束缚模型中识别出三种特征临界频率：配对频率、尖峰频率和截止频率，这些在线性化k·p模型中均不存在。尖峰频率和截止频率对带倾斜和狄拉克点移动变化具有鲁棒性，分别与布里渊区边界处的带间光学跃迁和泡利不相容原理相关。

Conclusion: 紧束缚模型中的特征临界频率反映了二维倾斜狄拉克系统的完整能带结构效应，这些频率在线性化k·p模型中缺失，表明完整能带描述对准确预测光学性质的重要性，为实验研究倾斜依赖的光学现象提供理论指导。

Abstract: Within the framework of linear response theory, we theoretically investigated the interband longitudinal optical conductivities (LOCs) in two-dimensional (2D) tilted Dirac bands using a tight-binding (TB) model, incorporating the effects of band tilting and Dirac-point shifting. We identified three characteristic critical frequencies in the interband LOCs of the TB model: the partner frequencies, the sharp- peak frequency, and the cutoff frequency. In contrast to conventional critical frequencies, these three types are consistently absent in the corresponding linearized $k\cdot p$ model. Notably, the sharp-peak frequency and cutoff frequency remain robust against variations in band tilting and Dirac-point shifting. By employing analytical expressions derived via the Lagrange multiplier method, we elucidate the origins of the conventional critical frequencies and their partner counterparts. In contrast, the sharp-peak frequency and cutoff frequency are associated with interband optical transitions at high-symmetry points of the energy bands, arising from the Pauli exclusion principle and the finite boundaries of the Brillouin zone. Our theoretical predictions are intended to guide future experimental studies on tilt-dependent optical phenomena in 2D tilted Dirac systems.

</details>


### [111] [Optically induced thermal demagnetization and switching of antiferromagnetic domains in NiO and CoO thin films](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05806)
*Maciej Dąbrowski,Tong Wu,Connor R. J. Sait,Jia Xu,Paul S. Keatley,Yizheng Wu,Robert J. Hicken,Olena Gomonay*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 利用激光热效应实现绝缘反铁磁材料中磁畴的全光学操控，通过移动激光束产生的温度梯度对畴壁施加热压力，实现可逆的90°磁畴切换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索无需电流的全光学方法来操控完全补偿的反铁磁材料，为超快光学操作开辟新途径，对非易失性存储技术和反铁磁自旋电子学有潜在影响。

Method: 使用磁光双折射成像技术，通过移动激光束在NiO/Pt和CoO/Pt薄膜中产生温度梯度，利用热压力驱动畴壁运动，实现反铁磁畴的光学操控。

Result: 单激光脉冲可热退磁并随机重分布磁畴；移动激光束可诱导受控畴壁运动，实现部分反铁磁序切换；通过反转热梯度方向可逆切换90°磁畴。

Conclusion: 建立了通过温度梯度产生的热压力实现完全补偿反铁磁材料超快光学操控的新途径，为无电流全光学开关提供了可行方案。

Abstract: We demonstrate all-optical manipulation of magnetic domains in NiO/Pt and CoO/Pt thin films with insulating antiferromagnetic layers. Using magneto-optical birefringence imaging, we show that even a single laser pulse can thermally demagnetize the antiferromagnet, leading to a random redistribution of domains. By sweeping the laser beam, controlled domain wall motion is induced, enabling partial switching of the antiferromagnetic order. The behavior is captured by an analytical model in which temperature gradients generated by the moving beam exert a thermal pressure on domain walls in the form of a ponderomotive force. Importantly, the 90$^{\circ}$ domains can be reversibly toggled solely by reversing the direction of the thermal gradient, demonstrating all-optical switching without the need for electric currents. These findings establish a route toward ultrafast optical manipulation of fully compensated antiferromagnets, with potential impact on non-volatile memory technologies and antiferromagnetic spintronics.

</details>


### [112] [Disentangling High Harmonic Generation from Surface and Bulk States of a Topological Insulator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06051)
*Sha Li,Wenyi Zhou,Kazi A. Imroz,Yaguo Tang,Tiana A. Townsend,Vyacheslav Leshchenko,Larissa Boie,Pierre Agostini,Alexandra S. Landsman,Roland K. Kawakami,Lun Yue,Louis F. DiMauro*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 通过调控Bi₂Se₃薄膜厚度和施加太赫兹扰动场，实现了对三维拓扑绝缘体中体态和表面态对高次谐波产生贡献的分离与控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三维拓扑绝缘体具有独特的体态绝缘、表面态导电的特性，但如何可靠地从高次谐波产生中提取拓扑特征存在争议。需要开发有效方法分离体态和表面态的光学响应。

Method: 使用不同厚度（6nm和50nm）的Bi₂Se₃薄膜样品，通过调节厚度控制体态和表面态对高次谐波产生的贡献。施加准静态太赫兹扰动场来解耦体态和表面态的响应，分析表面态的位移矢量和贝里曲率对高次谐波产生的影响。

Result: 超薄（6nm）薄膜显著增强了表面态对高次谐波产生的贡献，而较厚（50nm）薄膜中体态主导谐波发射。太赫兹扰动场成功分离了体态和表面态的响应，揭示了表面态位移矢量和贝里曲率对高次谐波产生的显著影响。

Conclusion: 研究提供了分离拓扑表面态与体态光学响应的有效方法，为解决能否可靠提取高次谐波产生中拓扑特征的争议开辟了途径，为研究拓扑材料的非线性光学性质提供了新工具。

Abstract: The discovery of topological phases has introduced a new dimension to materials science. Three-dimensional (3D) topological insulators (TIs) are a remarkable class of matter that is insulating in the bulk while hosting conductive topological surface states (TSSs) with unique charge and spin properties. High-order harmonic generation (HHG) has emerged as a powerful tool to probe condensed matter systems by providing insights into their electronic structure and dynamic behavior. Here, we investigate HHG in the prototype 3D-TI Bi$_2$Se$_3$. We demonstrate that the contributions of bulk and surface states to the harmonic emission can be controlled by tuning the thickness of thin film samples. An ultrathin (6 nm) film substantially enhances HHG from the surface states, while the bulk states dominate HHG in a thicker (50 nm) film. By applying a quasi-static terahertz perturbing field, we disentangle the bulk and surface responses and reveal the significant impact of the surface states' shift vector and Berry curvature on HHG. Our study provides effective methods for isolating the optical responses of TSSs from those of the bulk, which opens the door to resolving an ongoing debate regarding whether it is possible to reliably extract topological signatures in HHG.

</details>


### [113] [Solving the Peierls-Boltzmann transport equation with matrix product states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06153)
*Sangyeop Lee,Hirad Alipanah,Juan José Mendoza-Arenas*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该论文提出使用矩阵乘积态（MPS）求解Peierls-Boltzmann输运方程，通过优化张量链排序和采用无量纲形式，显著提高了维度约简效果，在硅晶体中实现了从弹道到扩散输运的精确模拟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Peierls-Boltzmann输运方程（PBE）因其包含实空间和模态空间的高维相空间而面临维度灾难问题。传统数值方法计算成本高昂，需要开发高效的维度约简技术来模拟非平衡声子输运。

Method: 采用矩阵乘积态（MPS）表示PBE的解，提出基于散射事件的MPS配置和无量纲形式以增强张量间相关性局部性。优化MPS索引排序，使实空间和模态空间的张量链从最粗网格向中心连接。结合密度矩阵重整化群启发的求解器，对有限体积法离散的PBE进行求解。

Result: 在硅晶体中成功求解了弹道、准弹性和扩散输运三种机制下的PBE。压缩比为10⁻³的MPS截断足以高保真地复现参考解。计算成本在实空间和模态空间均呈亚线性增长，相比稀疏矩阵运算的有限体积法，计算时间减少约一个数量级。

Conclusion: MPS方法能有效缓解PBE的维度灾难问题，通过优化配置实现高效维度约简。该方法为高维相空间输运问题提供了有前景的数值框架，计算效率显著优于传统方法。

Abstract: The Peierls-Boltzmann transport equation (PBE), which governs non-equilibrium phonon transport, suffers from the curse of dimensionality due to its high-dimensional phase space including both real and modal spaces. We explore the use of matrix product states (MPS) for numerical simulation of the PBE. We show that an MPS configuration based on scattering events combined with a dimensionless form of the solution can drastically increase the locality of correlations between tensors in the MPS representation, enhancing its effectiveness in dimension reduction. We further examine the effects of index ordering in an MPS and find that the highest locality is achieved when tensor chains associated with both real and modal spaces are connected from the coarsest grid to each other in the center of the MPS. Using this optimal configuration and a solver inspired by the density matrix renormalization group, we solve the PBE discretized by a finite volume method (FVM). The solution is obtained for crystalline silicon across ballistic, quasi-ballistic, and diffusive transport regimes. An MPS truncated to the compression ratio of $10^{-3}$ suffices to reproduce reference solutions with high fidelity. The computational cost scales sublinearly with the number of grid points in both real and modal spaces, achieving roughly an order of magnitude reduction in computational time compared to the FVM with sparse matrix operation.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.stat-mech'></div>

# cond-mat.stat-mech [[Back]](#toc)

### [114] [Emergent symmetry and thermodynamic crossovers for supercritical AdS black holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04968)
*Zhong-Ying Fan*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种基于Lee-Yang相变理论的新方法，用于研究超临界AdS黑洞的交叉线，揭示了复杂相空间中涌现的Ising对称性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Ising对称性通常在液-气相之间的临界区域涌现，这种普适性对超临界流体的热力学交叉行为施加了强约束。作者旨在将Lee-Yang相变理论应用于超临界AdS黑洞，以探索其交叉线行为

Method: 开发了一种新颖方法，通过保持模压力为实数，将Lee-Yang零点解析延拓到超临界区域的复平面中，从而获得一对复交叉线。然后将这些复交叉线投影到实相空间来定义实交叉线

Result: 获得了一对表现出普适标度的复交叉线，在复相空间中展现了涌现的Ising对称性。通过投影得到的实交叉线将临界点以上的相图划分为三个不同的区域：类液态、不可区分态和类气态

Conclusion: 该方法揭示了超临界AdS黑洞相图中存在多个交叉线，与仅包含单一交叉线的传统情景形成鲜明对比，证明了Ising对称性在复杂相空间中的涌现

Abstract: Ising symmetry typically emerges in the critical domain between liquid-gas phases. Universality of this property imposes strong constraints on the behavior of thermodynamic crossovers for supercritical fluids. In this work, we develop a novel approach to investigate the crossover lines for supercritical AdS black holes using Lee-Yang phase transition theory. We analytically continue Lee-Yang zeros into the complex plane within the supercritical region by keeping a modular pressure real. Consequently, we obtain a pair of complex crossover lines, which exhibit universal scalings and manifest the emergent Ising symmetry in the complex phase space. The real crossover lines are defined by projecting the complex crossovers onto the real phase space. As a result, the phase diagram above the critical point is divided into three distinct regimes: liquid-like, indistinguishable and gas-like states, in sharp contrast to scenarios featuring only a single crossover line.

</details>


### [115] [Quantum state randomization constrained by non-Abelian symmetries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05043)
*Yuhan Wu,Joaquin F. Rodriguez-Nieva*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 非阿贝尔对称性约束下的量子系统，即使经历长时间幺正演化，也难以从低纠缠初态产生真正的随机态，实验初态限制而非动力学本身限制了Haar随机化程度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究幺正量子动力学如何产生随机性这一跨学科核心问题，特别关注物理系统普遍存在的对称性约束（从简单标量对称性到复杂非阿贝尔对称性）如何限制希尔伯特空间可达区域并阻碍纯随机态生成。

Method: 分析具有非对易对称性（如SU(2)）的系统，研究幺正动力学下可实现的Haar类随机化程度如何受实验初态限制（特别是低纠缠初态）而非对称性约束动力学本身限制。证明时间演化态原则上可在有限统计矩层面再现Haar类行为，前提是初态与Haar系综中守恒算符的对应矩匹配。

Result: 对于可编程量子系统中常用的无纠缠初态，无法满足Haar随机化条件。即使在强量子混沌区域渐近长时间下，晚期态在纠缠熵等探针中仍可与Haar随机态区分，偏离Haar行为随系统尺寸增大保持有限。量化了可达到的最大纠缠熵，并识别了产生最熵晚期态的无纠缠初态条件。

Conclusion: 非阿贝尔对称性结构与实验态制备约束的结合会强烈限制晚期可实现的Haar类随机化程度，表明实验初态限制而非动力学本身是随机化过程的主要瓶颈。

Abstract: The emergence of randomness from unitary quantum dynamics is a central problem across diverse disciplines, ranging from the foundations of statistical mechanics to quantum algorithms and quantum computation. Physical systems are invariably subject to constraints -- from simple scalar symmetries to more complex non-Abelian ones -- that restrict the accessible regions of Hilbert space and obstruct the generation of pure random states. In this work, we show that for systems with noncommuting symmetries such as SU(2), the degree of Haar-like randomization achievable under unitary dynamics is strongly constrained by experimental limitations on state initialization, in particular low-entanglement initial states, rather than by the symmetry-constrained dynamics themselves. Specifically, we show that time-evolved states can, in principle, reproduce Haar-like behavior at the level of finite statistical moments (i.e., those accessible under realistic experimental conditions with a finite number of state copies) provided that the initial state matches the corresponding moments of the conserved operators in the Haar ensemble. However, for the unentangled initial states commonly used in programmable quantum systems, this condition cannot be satisfied. Consequently, even at asymptotically long times in strongly quantum-chaotic regimes, late-time states remain distinguishable from Haar-random states in probes such as entanglement entropy, with deviations from Haar behavior that remain finite with increasing system size. We quantify the maximal entanglement entropy achievable and identify the unentangled initial conditions that yield the most entropic late-time states. Our results show that the combination of non-Abelian symmetry structure and experimental constraints on state preparation can strongly limit the degree of Haar-like randomization achievable at late times.

</details>


### [116] [Nematic Phase Transitions and Density Modulations in 1D Flat Band Condensates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05258)
*Yeongjun Kim,Oleg I. Utesov,Alexei Andreanov,Mikhail V. Fistul,Sergej Flach*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 研究一维Gross-Pitaevskii平带晶格的基态性质，发现几何驱动的向列相变，揭示平带几何对玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚态非平凡相的影响


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究平带晶格系统中几何效应对玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚态基态性质的影响，探索几何参数如何驱动相变并产生非平凡凝聚态相

Method: 采用全带平模型，分析Gross-Pitaevskii方程，使用Bogoliubov-de Gennes激发理论和模拟退火方法，研究锯齿晶格等具体系统

Result: 发现几何驱动的向列相变，当几何参数θ≥π/8时，无限小在位相互作用即可破坏均匀凝聚态；在临界点θ=π/4处识别出密度调制基态；证明这些相通过无序有序机制在低温下被热选择

Conclusion: 平带几何能够驱动非平凡凝聚态相的产生，声速可作为探测几何相结构的敏感探针，这些发现对理解平带系统中的玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚态具有重要意义

Abstract: We investigate the ground-state properties of one-dimensional Gross-Pitaevskii flat-band lattices. We uncover a geometry-driven phase transition into a macroscopically degenerate nematic state with broken time reversal symmetry. Focusing on all-bands-flat (ABF) models, we demonstrate that even infinitesimal onsite interactions can destabilize a uniform, constant-phase condensate, driving the system into a nematic manifold as the flat-band geometry controlled parameter $θ\geq π/8$. At a critical endpoint (\(θ=π/4\)), where the compact localized states exhibit constant amplitudes, we identify an additional pair of density-modulated ground states characterized by vanishing phase stiffness. Utilizing Bogoliubov-de Gennes excitations and simulated annealing, we show that these density-modulated phases are thermally selected at low temperatures via an order-by-disorder mechanism. Finally, we demonstrate that these non-trivial condensate phases extend beyond ABF models, as exemplified by the sawtooth lattice. Our findings also reveal that the sound velocity in flat-band condensates is a sensitive probe of the underlying geometric phase structure.

</details>


### [117] [Entanglement in the open XX chain: Rényi oscillations, hard-edge crossover, and symmetry resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05356)
*Miguel Tierz*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该论文推导了开放边界XX自旋1/2链的Rényi纠缠熵的封闭形式渐近公式，通过将边界相关矩阵行列式映射到具有正权重的Hankel行列式，并利用Riemann-Hilbert方法获得大尺寸渐近行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究开放边界条件下量子自旋链的纠缠熵行为，特别是边界效应如何影响纠缠熵的渐近形式、振荡振幅和相位，以及对称分辨熵的分布特性。

Method: 将边界相关矩阵（具有Toeplitz-plus-Hankel结构）的行列式映射到具有正权重的Hankel行列式，利用已知的Riemann-Hilbert结果推导大尺寸渐近公式，并通过Szegő函数显式计算振荡振幅和相位。

Result: 获得了封闭形式的渐近公式，发现单个变量s=2ℓsin(k_F/2)能组织硬边交叉行为，振荡包络服从s^{±1/α}幂律，ln s是数据塌缩的自然主导对数。对于分离块，振荡振幅与共形交叉比因子化一致。同时恢复了开放边界条件下对称分辨熵的均分偏移-½log log ℓ，以及相对于周期链的高斯宽度减半。

Conclusion: 该工作为开放边界量子自旋链的纠缠熵提供了完整的渐近描述，揭示了边界效应如何系统地修改纠缠特性，包括振荡行为、交叉标度和对称分辨熵分布，建立了统一的理论框架。

Abstract: We derive closed-form asymptotic formulas for the Rényi entanglement entropies of the open XX spin-$1/2$ chain by mapping the underlying determinant of the boundary correlation matrix (which has Toeplitz-plus-Hankel structure) to a Hankel determinant with a positive weight whose large-size asymptotics follow from known Riemann--Hilbert results. An explicit evaluation of the Szegő function yields the leading $2k_F$ oscillatory amplitude and phase. A single variable $s = 2\ell \sin(k_F/2)$ organizes the hard-edge crossover as the Fermi momentum approaches the band edge: the oscillation envelope obeys $s^{\pm1/α}$ power laws and $\ln s$ is the natural leading logarithm for a clean data collapse. For detached blocks the oscillatory amplitude is numerically consistent with a factorization through the conformal cross-ratio. The same framework recovers the open-boundary-condition (OBC) equipartition offset $-\tfrac{1}{2}\log\log\ell$ for symmetry-resolved entropies, together with the known halving of the Gaussian width relative to the periodic chain.

</details>


### [118] [Quantitative analysis of fluctuating hydrodynamics in uniform shear flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05474)
*Hiroyoshi Nakano,Yuki Minami*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该研究通过直接数值模拟验证了两种经典涨落流体动力学理论的定量预测能力：Lutsko-Dufty非平衡长程关联理论和FNS动态重整化群理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 均匀剪切流下涨落流体动力学的理论预测缺乏精确的定量验证，因为解析近似的影响难以先验评估，且微观粒子模拟存在局限性。

Method: 采用剪切周期边界条件，对涨落Navier-Stokes方程进行直接数值模拟：1) 线性化方程验证Lutsko-Dufty理论；2) 完整非线性方程验证FNS动态重整化群理论。

Result: Lutsko-Dufty理论的预测在从粘性主导的长波区域到剪切主导的短波区域都定量有效，远超其原始假设范围；FNS的单圈重整化群预测在强非线性区域仍保持定量准确性，而传统微扰理论在此区域失效。

Conclusion: 研究结果巩固了这些经典理论的基础，为使用涨落流体动力学进行定量分析铺平了道路。

Abstract: Many theoretical predictions in fluctuating hydrodynamics under uniform shear flow have lacked precise quantitative verification due to analytical approximations whose quantitative impacts are difficult to assess a priori and the limitations of microscopic particle-based simulations. To address this problem, we perform direct numerical simulations (DNS) of the fluctuating Navier-Stokes (NS) equations with shear-periodic boundary conditions. We provide a decisive quantitative validation of two seminal frameworks: the Lutsko-Dufty theory for nonequilibrium long-range correlations, and the dynamic renormalization group (RG) theory by Forster, Nelson, and Stephen (FNS) for anomalous transport. By simulating the linearized fluctuating NS equations, we demonstrate that the predictions of the Lutsko-Dufty theory are quantitatively valid from the viscous-dominated, long-wavelength regime to the shear-dominated, short-wavelength regime, well beyond their originally assumed limits. Moving beyond the linearized equations, we simulate the full nonlinear fluctuating NS equations to test the quantitative predictive capability of the dynamical RG approach by FNS. Our results show that the one-loop RG prediction remains quantitatively accurate up to a strongly nonlinear regime, where conventional perturbation theory fails. Our findings solidify the foundations of these classical theories, paving the way for quantitative analyses using fluctuating hydrodynamics.

</details>


### [119] [Exact solution of three-point functions in critical loop models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05503)
*Morris Ang,Gefei Cai,Jesper Lykke Jacobsen,Rongvoram Nivesvivat,Paul Roux,Xin Sun,Baojun Wu*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 提出了临界环模型中球面上三点函数的精确公式，并通过三种方法验证：共形自举法、转移矩阵研究和概率方法


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决临界环模型中的关键缺失部分，揭示二维统计物理中三种基本方法（转移矩阵、共形场论和概率论）之间的深层统一性

Method: 1. 使用共形自举法分析四点函数；2. 通过转移矩阵研究格点模型；3. 基于共形环系综和Liouville量子引力的概率方法

Result: 提出了主场$V_{(r,s)}$的三点函数的精确公式，其中$2r$表示腿数，参数$s$描述$r=0$时的对角场和$r>0$时的腿动量

Conclusion: 该工作为求解临界环模型提供了关键缺失部分，揭示了转移矩阵、共形场论和概率论三种方法在二维统计物理中的深层统一性

Abstract: We propose an exact formula for three-point functions on the sphere in critical loop models with primary fields $V_{(r,s)}$ characterized by $2r$ legs and a parameter \(s\) that describes diagonal fields for $r=0$ and the momentum of legs for $r>0$. We demonstrate its validity in three ways: the conformal bootstrap method for 4-point functions, a transfer-matrix study of the lattice model, and a probabilistic method based on conformal loop ensemble and Liouville quantum gravity. This work provides a crucial missing piece for solving critical loop models and reveals a deep unity between three fundamental approaches to 2D statistical physics: transfer matrix, conformal field theory, and probability theory.

</details>


### [120] [Shortcuts to state transitions for active matter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05585)
*Guodong Cheng,Z. C. Tu,Geng Li*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 开发了一种用于弱活性系统的捷径框架，通过引入辅助势能实现有限时间内的状态转换，并基于耗散功推导出几何优化方法


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统捷径方案主要针对被动系统，而活性系统由于自由能消耗驱动的定向运动持续远离平衡态，需要专门框架来处理弱活性条件下的快速状态转换问题

Method: 引入辅助势能引导系统沿预定分布路径演化；基于耗散功定义热力学度量，在控制参数空间诱导黎曼流形；最优协议对应几何空间中的测地线路径；对两种内部势能系统分别采用精确推导和变分近似方法

Result: 成功构建了活性系统的捷径框架，在外部谐波势阱约束下，对两种内部相互作用系统实现了有限时间状态转换；测地线协议相比线性协议能有效减少耗散

Conclusion: 该工作为弱活性系统提供了通用的捷径框架，通过几何优化方法最小化耗散功，为活性物质系统的非平衡控制提供了新工具

Abstract: Shortcut schemes can accelerate quasi-static processes in passive systems by adding auxiliary controls to realize swift transitions between equilibrium states. In active systems, however, inherently directed motion driven by free energy consumption continually drives the system away from equilibrium. In this work, we develop a shortcut framework to realize swift state transitions for active systems operating in the weak activity regime. An auxiliary potential is introduced to guide the system along a predefined distribution path, allowing it to reach the target state within a finite time. Considering unavoidable energy cost in such a finite-time process, we derive a thermodynamic metric from the dissipative work to induce a Riemann manifold on the space spanned by the control parameters. The optimal protocol with minimum dissipative work is then identical to the geodesic path in the geometric space. We demonstrate this framework by considering active systems confined in an external harmonic trap and interacting via two distinct internal potentials, respectively: an attractive harmonic coupling and a repulsive pairwise Gaussian-core coupling. The strengths of both the external trap and the internal interactions are controllable. For the latter case, since the auxiliary potential can not be derived precisely, we adopt a variational method to obtain an approximate auxiliary control. Compared to linear protocols, the geodesic protocols can effectively reduce dissipation.

</details>


### [121] [Percolation in the three-dimensional Ising model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05772)
*Jinhong Zhu,Tao Chen,Zhiyi Li,Sheng Fang,Youjin Deng*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 三维临界Ising模型中几何自旋团簇仅存在单一渗流相变，不同于二维中的双重相变；嵌入三维临界Ising模型中的二维层系统表现出由平面外临界关联诱导的新普适类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前研究发现二维临界Ising模型中几何自旋团簇存在两个连续的渗流相变，本研究旨在探究这一现象在三维临界Ising模型中是否仍然存在，并研究嵌入三维临界Ising模型中的二维层系统的渗流特性。

Method: 采用大规模蒙特卡洛模拟研究三维临界Ising模型的几何自旋团簇渗流行为；对完全图上的Ising模型进行理论分析；研究嵌入三维临界Ising模型中的二维层系统，估计红键指数和分形维数。

Result: 三维临界Ising模型中仅观察到单一渗流相变，与二维情况不同；完全图上的理论分析得到相同结论；二维层系统的红键指数y_p=0.426(6)，最大团簇、边界和最短路径的分形维数分别为d_f=1.8926(20)、d_hull=1.663(4)、d_min=1.080(10)，表明由平面外临界关联诱导的新普适类。

Conclusion: 三维临界Ising模型中几何自旋团簇的双重渗流相变现象消失，仅存在单一相变；嵌入三维临界Ising模型中的二维层系统表现出独特的渗流临界行为，形成新的普适类，其临界指数与标准二维渗流不同。

Abstract: Geometric representations provide a useful perspective on critical phenomena in the Ising model. In a recent study [Phys. Rev. E 112, 034118 (2025)], we found that the two-dimensional critical Ising model exhibits two consecutive percolation transitions for geometric spin clusters as the bond-occupation probability $p$ between parallel spins increases. Here, through extensive Monte Carlo simulations, we show that this phenomenon does not persist in three dimensions, where we observe only a single percolation transition on critical Ising configurations. Further theoretical analysis of the Ising model on the complete graph also yields the same scenario. In addition, we study percolation on a two-dimensional layer embedded in the three-dimensional critical Ising model. For this layer system, we estimate the red-bond exponent $y_p = 0.426(6)$ and the fractal dimensions of the largest cluster, hull, and shortest path as $d_f = 1.8926(20)$, $d_{\rm hull} = 1.663(4)$, and $d_{\rm min} = 1.080(10)$, respectively. These values indicate a distinct universality class induced by coupling to out-of-plane critical correlations.

</details>


### [122] [Generalized hydrodynamics of free fermions under extensive-charge monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05850)
*Pablo Bayona-Pena,Michele Mazzoni,Lorenzo Piroli*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 研究自由费米子在广延区域守恒荷外部监测下的输运动力学，通过广义流体动力学框架分析二分协议中的局域电荷和电流分布


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索在广延区域守恒荷监测下量子系统的输运特性，特别是理解监测如何影响自由费米子系统的动力学行为，为研究相互作用系统在类似条件下的输运奠定基础

Method: 采用广义流体动力学（GHD）框架，结合数值-解析混合方法，研究二分协议中监测半系统总粒子数的情况，将非局域Lindbladian动力学理解为局域杂质问题

Result: 监测导致分布中出现不连续性，随着监测速率增加不连续性变得更加明显，在无限监测速率的Zeno极限下输运完全消失；GHD框架成功描述了这些现象

Conclusion: 提出的GHD框架为系统研究可积模型在广延荷测量下的输运提供了有效途径，可自然扩展到相互作用系统，揭示了监测对量子输运的显著影响

Abstract: We study transport dynamics of free fermions subject to the external monitoring of a conserved charge over an extensive region. Focusing on bipartition protocols, we consider monitoring the total particle number over half of the system, and study the profiles of local charges and currents at hydrodynamic scales. While the Lindbladian describing the averaged dynamics is non-local, we show that the profiles can be understood in terms of localized impurities. We present a general framework based on the generalized hydrodynamics (GHD) picture, allowing for a hybrid numerical-analytic solution of the quench dynamics at hydrodynamic scales. We illustrate our approach for domain-wall initial states, showing that monitoring leads to discontinuities in the profiles that become more pronounced as the rate increases and that lead to the absence of transport in the Zeno limit of infinite monitoring rates. Our GHD framework could be naturally extended to interacting systems, paving the way for a systematic study of transport of integrable models subject to extensive-charge measurements.

</details>


### [123] [Comment on "Inferring the Dynamics of Underdamped Stochastic Systems"](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06037)
*Yeeren I. Low*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该论文是对Brückner等人(2020)工作的纠错说明，指出了原论文在推导欠阻尼朗之万方程系统动力学推断方法时存在的若干重要错误


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Brückner等人(2020)提出了一种在测量噪声存在下推断欠阻尼朗之万方程系统动力学的新方法，但该论文包含多个重要错误。本纠错说明旨在解释并修正这些错误，确保方法的正确性和可靠性

Method: 通过分析Brückner等人(2020)论文中的数学推导和理论框架，识别其中的错误，并提供正确的数学推导和理论解释。采用纠错说明的形式，系统性地指出原论文中的问题并提供修正方案

Result: 成功识别了Brückner等人(2020)论文中的多个重要错误，包括数学推导错误、理论假设问题或方法应用限制等，并提供了相应的修正方案，确保欠阻尼朗之万方程系统动力学推断方法的正确性

Conclusion: 虽然Brückner等人(2020)的工作在欠阻尼朗之万系统动力学推断方面具有重要贡献，但其中包含的数学和理论错误需要修正。本纠错说明提供了必要的修正，有助于完善该方法并确保其在相关领域的正确应用

Abstract: D. B. Brückner et al. [Phys. Rev. Lett. 125, 058103 (2020)] have described a novel method for inferring the dynamics of systems governed by an underdamped Langevin equation in the presence of measurement noise. While this is a significant achievement, the paper also presents a number of significant errors. These are explained and corrected in this note.

</details>


### [124] [Dynamical phase diagram of synchronization in one dimension: universal behavior from Edwards-Wilkinson to random deposition through Kardar-Parisi-Zhang](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06040)
*Ricardo Gutierrez,Rodolfo Cuerno*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该研究通过数值模拟揭示了二维相位振荡器同步动力学中的通用标度不变性，展示了从Edwards-Wilkinson到Kardar-Parisi-Zhang行为的交叉转变，并分析了相位滑移对缩放行为的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然一维同步已被证明具有KPZ普适类的通用标度不变性，但其鲁棒性和参数空间边界仍不明确，这阻碍了向高维扩展和实验观测非平衡临界性。本研究旨在通过完整数值相图阐明同步动力学的精确边界和动态特征。

Method: 基于具有时间相关噪声或柱状（淬火）无序的相位振荡器系统，进行完整数值相图分析，包括饱和时间和数值，重点关注同步动力学逐渐出现的动态特征。

Result: 在非同步区域，动力学表现为随机沉积（时间相关噪声）或线性增长（柱状无序）；在同步区域，随着无序强度或振荡器耦合非奇性的增加，观察到从Edwards-Wilkinson到Kardar-Parisi-Zhang行为的交叉转变，其组合效应部分由KPZ耦合捕获。同时分析了去同步边界附近相位滑移导致的标度畸变。

Conclusion: 研究提供了二维同步动力学的完整数值相图，揭示了从EW到KPZ行为的交叉转变机制，并指出相位滑移在实验环境中的重要性，为理解同步临界性和向高维扩展奠定了基础。

Abstract: Synchronization in one dimension displays generic scale invariance with universal properties previously observed in surface kinetic roughening and the wider context of the Kardar-Parisi-Zhang (KPZ) universality class. This has been established for phase oscillators and also for some limit-cycle oscillators, both in the presence of columnar (quenched) disorder and of time-dependent noise, by extensive numerical simulations, and has been analytically motivated by continuum approximations in the strong oscillator coupling limit. The robustness and the precise boundaries in parameter space for such critical behavior remain unclear, however, which may preclude further developments, including the extension of these results to higher dimensions and the experimental observation of nonequilibrium criticality in synchronizing (e.g.~electronic or chemical) oscillators. We here present complete numerical phase diagrams of one-dimensional synchronization, including saturation times and values, but, most importantly, also dynamical features giving insight into the gradual emergence of synchronous dynamics, based on systems of phase oscillators with either type of randomness. In the absence of synchronization, the dynamics evolves as expected for random deposition (for time-dependent noise) or linear growth (for columnar disorder), while a crossover from Edwards-Wilkinson to Kardar-Parisi-Zhang behavior (with the corresponding type of randomness) is observed as the randomness strength, or the nonoddity of the coupling among oscillators, is increased in the synchronous region -- their combined effect being partially captured by the so-called KPZ coupling. The distortion of scaling due to phase slips near the desynchronization boundary, a feature that is likely to play a role in experimental contexts, is also discussed.

</details>


### [125] [Mutual Linearity in and out of Stationarity for Markov Jump Processes: A Trajectory-Based Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06162)
*Jiming Zheng,Zhiyue Lu*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文通过轨迹层面的线性响应理论推导了马尔可夫跳跃过程中的互线性关系，并将其推广到非平稳弛豫动力学中的状态可观测量和计数可观测量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 互线性关系是理解非平衡系统响应特性的重要理论框架，但先前研究主要基于线性代数分析且局限于特定情况。需要从轨迹层面推导互线性关系，以揭示其根本起源并推广到更广泛的系统。

Method: 采用轨迹层面的线性响应理论，通过分析单个边跃迁速率变化对轨迹概率的影响，推导互线性关系。这种方法不依赖于线性代数分析，而是基于轨迹概率的扰动。

Result: 成功从轨迹层面推导出互线性关系，并将其推广到非平稳弛豫动力学中的状态可观测量和计数可观测量。揭示了互线性关系的轨迹层面起源。

Conclusion: 轨迹层面的方法为互线性关系提供了更基础的推导，使其能够推广到更广泛的系统，包括扩散过程和开放量子系统，深化了对非平衡响应性质的理解。

Abstract: Nonequilibrium response theory is a fundamental framework for understanding how physical systems respond to perturbations. Recently, a mutual linearity has been discovered for Markov jump processes using linear algebra analysis. This mutual linearity states that two observables are linearly dependent on each other in the long-time limit when the transition rate of a single edge is altered. It has also been extended to non-stationary cases for current observables. In this work, we provide a trajectory-based derivation of mutual linearity utilizing the trajectory-level linear response theory. The trajectory approach allows us to generalize the mutual linearity to non-stationary relaxation dynamics for state observables and counting observables. Our results shed light on the fundamental response properties far from equilibrium and the trajectory-level origin of mutual linearity. Our trajectory-based approach makes it possible to generalize the mutual linearity to a broader class of systems, including diffusion processes and open quantum systems.

</details>


### [126] [From Near-Integrable to Far-from-Integrable: A Unified Picture of Thermalization and Heat Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.15566)
*Weicheng Fu,Zhen Wang,Yisen Wang,Yong Zhang,Hong Zhao*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 研究一维双原子硬点气体弛豫动力学，建立了涵盖从近可积到远可积区域的相图，揭示了热化与热输运的统一描述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Bogoliubov的三阶段演化假说为理解非平衡统计物理中的热化和输运提供了定性框架，但定量研究主要局限于近可积系统。需要研究更广泛的参数空间，从近可积到远可积区域，以全面理解弛豫动力学。

Method: 研究一维双原子硬点气体的弛豫动力学，分析热化（非平衡态中的局域能量弛豫）和热输运（平衡态中热流涨落的衰减）。建立相图来表征整个参数空间中的弛豫行为，包括近可积、远可积和中间区域。

Result: 发现三个普适动力学区域：(1) 近可积区域：动力学过程主导，局域能量弛豫指数衰减，热化时间τ∝δ^{-2}；(2) 远可积区域：流体动力学效应主导，能量弛豫幂律衰减，热化时间与系统尺寸N线性相关；(3) 中间区域：出现Bogoliubov相，特征为从动力学弛豫到流体动力学弛豫的转变。相图显示流体动力学行为可在小系统中出现，挑战了此类效应仅在大系统中发生的观点。热输运分析与热化结果一致，提供了热化与输运的统一理论描述。

Conclusion: 该研究为理解多体系统的弛豫动力学提供了新途径，建立了从近可积到远可积区域的完整相图，揭示了热化与热输运的统一性，并表明流体动力学效应可在小系统中出现。该方法可推广到量子系统等更广泛的多体系统研究。

Abstract: Whether and how a system approaches equilibrium is central in nonequilibrium statistical physics, crucial to understanding thermalization and transport. Bogoliubov's three-stage (initial, kinetic, and hydrodynamic) evolution hypothesis offers a qualitative framework, but quantitative progress has focused on near-integrable systems like dilute gases. In this work, we investigate the relaxation dynamics of a one-dimensional diatomic hard-point (DHP) gas, presenting a phase diagram that characterizes relaxation behavior across the full parameter space, from near-integrable to far-from-integrable regimes. We analyze thermalization (local energy relaxation in nonequilibrium states) and identify three universal dynamical regimes: (i) In the near-integrable regime, kinetic processes dominate, local energy relaxation decays exponentially, and the thermalization time $τ$ scales as $τ\propto δ^{-2}$. (ii) In the far-from-integrable regime, hydrodynamic effects dominate, energy relaxation decays power-law, and thermalization time scales linearly with system size $N$. (iii) In the intermediate regime, the Bogoliubov phase emerges, characterized by the transition from kinetic to hydrodynamic relaxation. The phase diagram also shows that hydrodynamic behavior can emerge in small systems when sufficiently far from the integrable regime, challenging the view that such effects occur only in large systems. In the thermodynamic limit, the system's relaxation depends on the order in which the limits ($N \to \infty$ or $δ\to 0$) are taken. We then analyze heat transport (decay of heat-current fluctuations in equilibrium), demonstrating its consistency with thermalization, leading to a unified theoretical description of thermalization and transport. Our approach provides a pathway for studying relaxation dynamics in many-body systems, including quantum systems.

</details>
